📚 本期文献进展 / Literature Brief

  • 本期狂犬病病毒研究集中呈现以下进展。
  • 有强有力的证据表明,在 COVID-19 期间,狂犬病 控制活动减少,导致贾夫纳的动物咬伤和 狂犬病 病例增加。狗 疫苗接种 覆盖率的增加与人类 狂犬病 死亡人数的减少相关。大多数 狂犬病 死亡 (93%) 发生在从事经济活动的成年人中,其中 64% 是男性,病例集中在瓦利卡马姆和瓦达马拉奇地区。
  • 66.7% 的患者和 62.9% 的患者将浸润难易程度评为“容易”(p = 0.63)。局部不良事件并不常见,疼痛发生率分别为 2.5% 和 7.5% (p = 0.07),红斑发生率≤ 1.3%。没有发生严重的全身不良事件。Rabishield 每位患者需要更多药瓶(2.28 ± 0.84 vs 2.00 ± 0.69;p = 0.001),并且平均浸润体积较高(4.7 ± 1.9 mL vs 4.0 ± 1.6 mL;p < 0.001),而组间浪费相似(1.03 ± 0.72 mL vs 1.03 ± 0.69 mL;p = 0.95)。
  • 单克隆抗体 (mAb) 是 RIG 的有效且经济的替代品。此外,最近的临床前研究提供的证据表明单克隆抗体可以有效治疗有症状的 狂犬病,克服病毒免疫逃避机制。 [10.1016/j.molmed.2026.06.014]。
  • 顺行追踪揭示了共享的和特定通路的二阶目标,包括双向 CA1v 预测。逆行追踪证实了预期的输入,例如 CA3,并确定了以前未识别的皮质来源,这些来源在 CA1v 亚群中存在差异。 [10.1007/s00429-026-03164-y]。
  • This reporting window highlights the following research developments.
  • There is strong evidence that rabies control activities were reduced during COVID-19, leading to increased animal bites and rabies cases in Jaffna.
  • Ease of infiltration was rated "easy" in 66.7% versus 62.9% of patients (p = 0.63). Local adverse events were uncommon, with pain reported in 2.5% versus 7.5% (p = 0.07) and erythema in ≤ 1.3% of participants.
  • Monoclonal antibodies (mAbs) represent an effective and economical alternative to RIGs.
  • Anterograde tracing revealed shared and pathway-specific second-order targets, including bidirectional CA1v projections.

  • 本期狂犬病病毒新闻集中呈现以下动态。
  • 已确认巴西政府通过泛美卫生组织向海地公共卫生部捐赠了 10,000 剂人用 狂犬病 疫苗。 [news-abaed3f836051992]。
  • 根据第 9482 号共和国法案(2007 年反 狂犬病 法案),确认全市范围内针对流浪狗的“Dakip-Aso”运动,以遏制 狂犬病 案件、咬伤事件和街头事故。 [news-4683bb2b82d1b747]。
  • 本文讨论了尼日利亚 狂犬病 的持续威胁,重点关注农牧民冲突及其对公共健康和安全的影响。它不报告任何确诊的 狂犬病 病例或 暴发。 [news-fa18470e9d7542a9]。
  • 在人类和动物中确认了 狂犬病 暴发。2026 年上半年,越南在 24 个省/市记录了 39 例人类 狂犬病 死亡和动物 暴发。卫生当局报告,与 2025 年同期相比,狂犬病、受感染动物、动物死亡和扑杀有所增加。数十人在被动物咬伤后需要进行 疫苗接种 接触后治疗。
  • This reporting window highlights the following Rabies virus news developments.
  • Confirmed donation of 10,000 doses of human rabies vaccine to Haiti's Ministry of Public Health by the Government of Brazil through PAHO. [news-abaed3f836051992].
  • Confirmed citywide 'Dakip-Aso' campaign against stray dogs under Republic Act 9482 (Anti-Rabies Act of 2007) to curb rabies cases, bite incidents, and street accidents. [news-4683bb2b82d1b747].
  • The article discusses the ongoing threat of rabies in Nigeria, focusing on the farmer-herder conflict and its impact on public health and safety. It does not report any confirmed rabies cases or outbreaks.
  • Confirmed rabies outbreak in humans and animals. Vietnam recorded 39 human rabies deaths and animal outbreaks in 24 provinces/cities in the first half of 2026.

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: PLOS Neglected Tropical Diseases  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-07-23  |  DOI: 10.1371/journal.pntd.0014274  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 20, 7, e0014274
C 补充信息学术文献 · 研究C supplementaryAcademic literature · Research

在资源有限的环境中,暴露后预防 单克隆抗体与混合 狂犬病 单克隆抗体的安全性和操作可行性

Safety and operational feasibility of single versus cocktail rabies monoclonal antibodies for post-exposure prophylaxis in a resource-limited setting
作者： Nidhi Fotedar, Haradanahalli Shankariah Ravish
摘要中文翻译背景:一旦出现症状,狂犬病 几乎都是致命的,但可以通过及时的 暴露后预防 (PEP) 来预防。全球人类和马 狂犬病 免疫球蛋白 (RIG) 短缺导致世界卫生组织推荐人类单克隆抗体 (mAb) 作为可接受的替代品。单抗体与双抗体混合单克隆抗体的真实世界数据比较有限,特别是在地方性、资源有限的环境中。方法:2021 年 10 月至 2022 年 12 月期间,在印度的一个大容量 狂犬病 PEP 诊所进行了一项比较观察性研究。连续接受 III 类动物暴露的患者接受单一人抗 狂犬病 mAb (Rabishield;n = 159) 或两种 mAb 混合物 (Twinrab;n = 159) 联合 WHO 推荐的药物疫苗。主要成果包括安全性、易于管理和操作可行性。使用独立 t 检验和卡方检验或 Fisher 精确检验对数据进行分析,p < 0.05 被认为是显着的。结果:Rabishield 组的平均年龄为 29.7 ± 19.4 岁,Twinrab 组的平均年龄为 32.8 ± 20.4 岁。66.7% 的患者和 62.9% 的患者将浸润难易程度评为“容易”(p = 0.63)。局部不良事件并不常见,疼痛发生率分别为 2.5% 和 7.5% (p = 0.07),红斑发生率≤ 1.3%。没有发生严重的全身不良事件。Rabishield 每位患者需要更多药瓶(2.28 ± 0.84 vs 2.00 ± 0.69;p = 0.001),并且平均浸润体积较高(4.7 ± 1.9 mL vs 4.0 ± 1.6 mL;p < 0.001),而组间浪费相似(1.03 ± 0.72 mL vs 1.03 ± 0.69 mL;p = 0.95)。结论:单一和混合人抗 狂犬病 单克隆抗体在程序化环境中都是安全的、耐受性良好且操作可行的。Rabishield 稍高的体积和小瓶要求可能会产生成本和物流影响。这些发现支持更广泛地采用单克隆抗体,以解决 狂犬病 流行、资源有限的环境中 RIG 短缺的问题。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
一旦出现症状,狂犬病 几乎都是致命的,但可以通过及时的 暴露后预防 (PEP) 来预防。全球人类和马 狂犬病 免疫球蛋白 (RIG) 短缺导致世界卫生组织推荐人类单克隆抗体 (mAb) 作为可接受的替代品。
设计与对象
2021 年 10 月至 2022 年 12 月期间,在印度的一个大容量 狂犬病 PEP 诊所进行了一项比较观察性研究。连续 III 类动物暴露的患者接受单一人抗 狂犬病 mAb (Rabishield;n = 159) 或两种 mAb 混合物 (Twinrab;n = 159) 与 WHO 推荐的 疫苗 的联合治疗。
核心方法
使用独立 t 检验和卡方检验或 Fisher 精确检验对数据进行分析,p < 0.05 被认为是显着的。主要成果包括安全性、易于管理和操作可行性。
主要结果
66.7% 的患者和 62.9% 的患者将浸润难易程度评为“容易”(p = 0.63)。局部不良事件并不常见,疼痛发生率分别为 2.5% 和 7.5% (p = 0.07),红斑发生率≤ 1.3%。没有发生严重的全身不良事件。Rabishield 每位患者需要更多药瓶(2.28 ± 0.84 vs 2.00 ± 0.69;p = 0.001),并且平均浸润体积较高(4.7 ± 1.9 mL vs 4.0 ± 1.6 mL;p < 0.001),而组间浪费相似(1.03 ± 0.72 mL vs 1.03 ± 0.69 mL;p = 0.95)。
解释与创新
单一和混合人抗 狂犬病 单克隆抗体在程序化环境中都是安全的、耐受性良好且操作可行的。Rabishield 稍高的体积和小瓶要求可能会产生成本和物流影响。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现支持更广泛地采用单克隆抗体,以解决 狂犬病 流行、资源有限的环境中 RIG 短缺的问题。
局限与证据强度
提供的证据没有报告长期疗效或 免疫应答,也没有提供有关所使用的特定单克隆抗体或其来源的详细信息。

Safety and operational feasibility of single versus cocktail rabies monoclonal antibodies for post-exposure prophylaxis in a resource-limited setting

Authors: Nidhi Fotedar, Haradanahalli Shankariah Ravish
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Rabies is almost universally fatal once symptoms develop but is preventable through timely post-exposure prophylaxis (PEP). Global shortages of human and equine rabies immunoglobulin (RIG) have led the World Health Organization to recommend human monoclonal antibodies (mAbs) as acceptable alternatives. Comparative real-world data on single-antibody versus two-antibody cocktail mAbs are limited, particularly from endemic, resource-limited settings. METHODS: A comparative observational study was conducted in a high-volume rabies PEP clinic in India between October 2021 and December 2022. Consecutive patients with category III animal exposures received either a single human anti-rabies mAb (Rabishield; n = 159) or a two-mAb cocktail (Twinrab; n = 159) in combination with WHO-recommended vaccine. Primary outcomes included safety, ease of administration, and operational feasibility. Data were analysed using independent t-tests and chi-square or Fisher's exact tests, with p < 0.05 considered significant. RESULTS: Mean age was 29.7 ± 19.4 years in the Rabishield group and 32.8 ± 20.4 years in the Twinrab group. Ease of infiltration was rated "easy" in 66.7% versus 62.9% of patients (p = 0.63). Local adverse events were uncommon, with pain reported in 2.5% versus 7.5% (p = 0.07) and erythema in ≤ 1.3% of participants. No serious systemic adverse events occurred. Rabishield required more vials per patient (2.28 ± 0.84 vs 2.00 ± 0.69; p = 0.001) and had a higher mean infiltration volume (4.7 ± 1.9 mL vs 4.0 ± 1.6 mL; p < 0.001), while wastage was similar between groups (1.03 ± 0.72 mL vs 1.03 ± 0.69 mL; p = 0.95). CONCLUSIONS: Both single and cocktail human anti-rabies mAbs were safe, well tolerated, and operationally feasible in a programmatic setting. The slightly higher volume and vial requirement for Rabishield may have cost and logistics implications. These findings support broader mAb adoption to address RIG shortages in rabies-endemic, resource-limited settings.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Rabies is almost universally fatal once symptoms develop but is preventable through timely post-exposure prophylaxis (PEP). Global shortages of human and equine rabies immunoglobulin (RIG) have led the World Health Organization to recommend human monoclonal antibodies (mAbs) as acceptable alternatives.
Design and population
A comparative observational study was conducted in a high-volume rabies PEP clinic in India between October 2021 and December 2022. Consecutive patients with category III animal exposures received either a single human anti-rabies mAb (Rabishield; n = 159) or a two-mAb cocktail (Twinrab; n = 159) in combination with WHO-recommended vaccine.
Core methods
Data were analysed using independent t-tests and chi-square or Fisher's exact tests, with p < 0.05 considered significant. Primary outcomes included safety, ease of administration, and operational feasibility.
Main results
Ease of infiltration was rated "easy" in 66.7% versus 62.9% of patients (p = 0.63). Local adverse events were uncommon, with pain reported in 2.5% versus 7.5% (p = 0.07) and erythema in ≤ 1.3% of participants. No serious systemic adverse events occurred. Rabishield required more vials per patient (2.28 ± 0.84 vs 2.00 ± 0.69; p = 0.001) and had a higher mean infiltration volume (4.7 ± 1.9 mL vs 4.0 ± 1.6 mL; p < 0.001), while wastage was similar between groups (1.03 ± 0.72 mL vs 1.03 ± 0.69 mL; p = 0.95).
Interpretation and novelty
Both single and cocktail human anti-rabies mAbs were safe, well tolerated, and operationally feasible in a programmatic setting. The slightly higher volume and vial requirement for Rabishield may have cost and logistics implications.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings support broader mAb adoption to address RIG shortages in rabies-endemic, resource-limited settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report on long-term efficacy or immune response, nor does it provide detailed information on the specific monoclonal antibodies used or their source.

腹侧海马动机控制系统的突触神经回路映射。

Transsynaptic neural circuit mapping of ventral hippocampus motivational control systems.
作者： Molly E. Klug, Mugil V Shanmugam, Haoyang Huang, Don Arnold, Joel D. Hahn, Scott E. Kanoski
摘要中文翻译腹侧海马体有助于食物摄入调节以及一系列动机和记忆过程,其功能障碍与多种认知和行为障碍有关。然而,其电路级组织仍不完全清楚。腹侧 CA1 (CA1v) 神经元将投射发送到涉及动机控制的多个区域。在这里,我们关注三个主要的前脑目标:伏隔核壳(ACBsh)、内侧前额叶皮层(mPFC)和外侧下丘脑区(LHA)。我们使用互补的跨突触顺行和逆行病毒追踪方法,绘制了 CA1v 神经元的上游和下游电路,这些神经元由它们对大鼠目标区域的投射来定义。使用 ATLAS 靶向 ACBsh、mPFC 和 LHA 的单突触输出,ATLAS 是一种新型的跨突触顺行病毒方法,可驱动接收来自 CA1v 的突触 传播 的神经元中的 Cre 重组酶。然后使用依赖于 Cre 的顺行病毒追踪策略绘制由这些定义的途径产生的二阶预测。同时,使用条件逆行糖蛋白删除的 狂犬病 病毒方法映射投射到每个下游靶标的 CA1v 神经元的上游输入。顺行追踪揭示了共享的和特定通路的二阶目标,包括双向 CA1v 预测。逆行追踪证实了预期的输入(例如 CA3),并发现了先前未识别的皮质源,这些源在下游投影定义的 CA1v 亚群中存在差异。总之,这些发现描绘了将 CA1v 神经元与关键激励系统连接起来的通路特异性多节点回路,这些回路可能为涉及腹侧海马功能障碍的疾病的未来治疗策略提供信息。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了对腹侧海马体回路水平组织的不完全理解,特别是其在动机控制系统和相关疾病中的作用。
设计与对象
该研究利用病毒追踪方法绘制了大鼠腹侧 CA1 (CA1v) 神经元的上游和下游回路,重点关注它们对伏隔核壳、内侧前额皮质和外侧下丘脑区域的投射。
核心方法
使用 ATLAS(一种跨突触顺行病毒方法)追踪目标区域的单突触输出,然后使用 Cre 依赖性顺行追踪进行二阶预测。使用条件逆行糖蛋白删除的 狂犬病 病毒方法绘制 CA1v 神经元的上游输入。
主要结果
顺行追踪揭示了共享的和特定通路的二阶目标,包括双向 CA1v 预测。逆行追踪证实了预期的输入,例如 CA3,并确定了以前未识别的皮质来源,这些来源在 CA1v 亚群中存在差异。
解释与创新
这些发现描绘了将 CA1v 神经元与关键激励系统连接起来的通路特异性、多节点回路,突出了对腹侧海马组织的新回路水平见解。
科研与公卫意义
所描绘的回路可能为未来涉及腹侧海马功能障碍的疾病的治疗策略提供信息,特别是那些与动机和认知障碍相关的疾病。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性在于其主要针对老鼠,可能无法完全推广到人类,并且使用的病毒追踪方法可能会引入技术偏差。证据是中等的,因为它提供了详细的电路图,但缺乏直接的功能或临床验证。

Transsynaptic neural circuit mapping of ventral hippocampus motivational control systems.

Authors: Molly E. Klug, Mugil V Shanmugam, Haoyang Huang, Don Arnold, Joel D. Hahn, Scott E. Kanoski
Original abstract (English)
The ventral hippocampus contributes to food intake regulation and a range of motivational and memory processes, and its dysfunction is associated with several cognitive and behavioral disorders. However, its circuit-level organization remains incompletely understood. Ventral CA1 (CA1v) neurons send projections to several regions involved in motivational control. Here we focus on three major forebrain targets: the nucleus accumbens shell (ACBsh), medial prefrontal cortex (mPFC), and lateral hypothalamic area (LHA). We mapped the upstream and downstream circuitry of CA1v neurons defined by their projections to these target regions in rats using complementary transsynaptic anterograde and retrograde viral tracing approaches. Monosynaptic outputs to ACBsh, mPFC, and LHA were targeted using ATLAS, a novel transsynaptic anterograde viral approach that drives Cre recombinase in neurons receiving synaptic transmission from the CA1v. Second-order projections arising from these defined pathways were then mapped using a Cre-dependent anterograde viral tracing strategy. In parallel, upstream inputs to CA1v neurons projecting to each downstream target were mapped using a conditional retrograde glycoprotein-deleted rabies viral approach. Anterograde tracing revealed both shared and pathway-specific second-order targets, including bidirectional CA1v projections. Retrograde tracing confirmed expected inputs (e.g., CA3) and uncovered previously unrecognized cortical sources that differed across downstream projection-defined CA1v subpopulations. Together, these findings delineate pathway-specific, multi-node circuits linking CA1v neurons to key motivational systems that may inform future therapeutic strategies for disorders involving ventral hippocampal dysfunction.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed the incomplete understanding of the ventral hippocampus's circuit-level organization, particularly its role in motivational control systems and associated disorders.
Design and population
The research mapped upstream and downstream circuitry of ventral CA1 (CA1v) neurons in rats, focusing on their projections to the nucleus accumbens shell, medial prefrontal cortex, and lateral hypothalamic area using viral tracing approaches.
Core methods
Monosynaptic outputs to target regions were traced using ATLAS, a transsynaptic anterograde viral method, followed by Cre-dependent anterograde tracing for second-order projections. Upstream inputs to CA1v neurons were mapped using a conditional retrograde glycoprotein-deleted rabies viral approach.
Main results
Anterograde tracing revealed shared and pathway-specific second-order targets, including bidirectional CA1v projections. Retrograde tracing confirmed expected inputs such as CA3 and identified previously unrecognized cortical sources that varied across CA1v subpopulations.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings delineate pathway-specific, multi-node circuits linking CA1v neurons to key motivational systems, highlighting novel circuit-level insights into ventral hippocampal organization.
Scientific and public-health significance
The delineated circuits may inform future therapeutic strategies for disorders involving ventral hippocampal dysfunction, particularly those linked to motivational and cognitive impairments.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its focus on rats, which may not fully generalize to humans, and the use of viral tracing methods that could introduce technical biases. The evidence is moderate, as it provides detailed circuit mapping but lacks direct functional or clinical validation.

在柬埔寨进行口头 狂犬病 疫苗接种 实地研究期间,当地狗群对诱饵接受度和 免疫应答 的评估

Evaluation of Bait Acceptance and Immune Response in a Local Dog Population During an Oral Rabies Vaccination Field Study in Cambodia
作者： Bengthay Tep, Sochetra Hak, Nouv Sophorn, Sin Grandy, AD de Vos, Dim Kanan, Kinzang Dukpa
摘要中文翻译(1) 背景:狗介导的人类 狂犬病 在柬埔寨仍然是一个严重的公共卫生问题,部分原因是狗的数量过多。由于大多数狗都是自由漫游的,不容易被处理和约束,因此 狂犬病 疫苗接种 的覆盖率非常低。口服 狂犬病 疫苗接种 (ORV) 可以将 疫苗接种 覆盖范围增加到中断 传播 周期所需的水平。(2)方法:本研究在柬埔寨茶雄省北部的当地散养狗身上测试了市售疫苗诱饵的诱饵接受性和免疫原性。(3) 结果:总共,75.4% (515/683) 的狗最初对含有 SPBN GASGAS 狂犬病病毒 疫苗 的蛋味诱饵表现出兴趣。随后,89.9%的狗食用了诱饵,几乎所有狗(98.3%)都被认为接种成功。在 疫苗接种 之前从 50 只狗身上采集了血液样本。这 50 只狗中的 8 只检测出 狂犬病 抗体 (ELISA) 血清阳性,并被排除在后续样本之外。疫苗接种 后 30 天和 90 天,重新安置的接种疫苗的狗的血清检测结果分别为 91.4% 和 68.2%。(4)结论:诱饵接受性和免疫原性与在其他国家使用相同疫苗诱饵进行的研究中观察到的结果相似。因此,在柬埔寨,ORV 似乎是控制狗 狂犬病 的一个非常有前途的工具,因为柬埔寨有大量拥有但自由漫游的狗。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
狗介导的人类 狂犬病 在柬埔寨仍然是一个严重的公共卫生问题,部分原因是狗的数量较多,而 狂犬病 覆盖率较低,因为大多数狗都是自由漫游且不易处理。
设计与对象
在这项研究中,在柬埔寨武雄省北部的当地自由漫步的狗身上测试了市售 疫苗 诱饵的诱饵接受性和免疫原性。
核心方法
在疫苗接种之前从50只狗身上采集了血液样本,并从后续样本中排除了8只血清阳性狗;使用含有 SPBN GASGAS 狂犬病病毒 疫苗 的蛋味诱饵评估诱饵接受度和免疫原性。
主要结果
总体而言,75.4% 的狗最初对诱饵表现出兴趣,89.9% 的狗食用了诱饵,98.3% 的狗被认为已成功接种疫苗;在 疫苗接种 后 30 天和 90 天,分别有 91.4% 和 68.2% 的接种犬检测出血清阳性。
解释与创新
作者解释说,诱饵接受度和免疫原性结果与在其他国家使用相同 疫苗 诱饵的研究中观察到的结果相似,表明 ORV 是柬埔寨狗 狂犬病 控制的有前途的工具。
科研与公卫意义
ORV 可以将 疫苗接种 覆盖率提高到中断柬埔寨 狂犬病 传播 周期所需的水平,柬埔寨拥有大量自养但自由漫游的狗群。
局限与证据强度
该研究是在柬埔寨的一个特定地区进行的,由于是单中心设计并且将血清呈阳性的狗排除在随访之外,因此证据强度中等。

Evaluation of Bait Acceptance and Immune Response in a Local Dog Population During an Oral Rabies Vaccination Field Study in Cambodia

Authors: Bengthay Tep, Sochetra Hak, Nouv Sophorn, Sin Grandy, AD de Vos, Dim Kanan, Kinzang Dukpa
Original abstract (English)
(1) Background: Dog-mediated human rabies remains a serious public health problem in Cambodia, partially due to the high abundance of dogs. As most dogs are free-roaming and cannot easily be handled and restrained, the rabies vaccination coverage is very low. Oral rabies vaccination (ORV) could increase the vaccination coverage to levels required for interrupting the transmission cycle. (2) Methods: In this study, the bait acceptance and immunogenicity of a commercially available vaccine bait were tested in local free-roaming dogs in the northern part of Takeo province in Cambodia. (3) Results: In total, 75.4% (515/683) of dogs initially showed interest when offered an egg-flavored bait containing the SPBN GASGAS rabies virus vaccine. Subsequently, 89.9% of these dogs consumed the bait, and almost all of them (98.3%) were considered successfully vaccinated. Blood samples were collected from 50 dogs prior to vaccination. Eight of these fifty dogs tested sero-positive for rabies antibodies (ELISA) and were excluded from follow-up samples. Thirty and ninety days post-vaccination, 91.4% and 68.2% of the relocated vaccinated dogs tested sero-positive, respectively. (4) Conclusions: Bait acceptance and immunogenicity showed similar results as observed in studies with the same vaccine bait conducted in other countries. Hence, ORV seems to be a very promising tool for dog rabies control in Cambodia, which has a large owned but free-roaming dog population.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dog-mediated human rabies remains a serious public health problem in Cambodia, partially due to the high abundance of dogs and low vaccination coverage because most dogs are free-roaming and cannot easily be handled.
Design and population
In this study, the bait acceptance and immunogenicity of a commercially available vaccine bait were tested in local free-roaming dogs in the northern part of Takeo province in Cambodia.
Core methods
Blood samples were collected from 50 dogs prior to vaccination, and eight sero-positive dogs were excluded from follow-up samples; bait acceptance and immunogenicity were assessed using an egg-flavored bait containing the SPBN GASGAS rabies virus vaccine.
Main results
In total, 75.4% of dogs initially showed interest when offered the bait, 89.9% consumed it, and 98.3% were considered successfully vaccinated; 91.4% and 68.2% of vaccinated dogs tested sero-positive at 30 and 90 days post-vaccination, respectively.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that bait acceptance and immunogenicity results were similar to those observed in studies with the same vaccine bait in other countries, indicating ORV is a promising tool for dog rabies control in Cambodia.
Scientific and public-health significance
ORV could increase vaccination coverage to levels required for interrupting the rabies transmission cycle in Cambodia, which has a large owned but free-roaming dog population.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was conducted in a specific region of Cambodia, and the evidence strength is moderate due to the single-site design and exclusion of sero-positive dogs from follow-up.

狂犬病 尽管在高负担环境中进行暴露后预防:疫苗 医疗服务中的失败或差距?

Rabies Despite Postexposure Prophylaxis in High-Burden Settings: Vaccine Failure or Gaps in Healthcare Delivery?
作者： Lonika Lodha, Shipra Gupta, M A Ashwini, Shubhangi Chandel, Reeta S Mani
摘要中文翻译背景:狂犬病 是一种致命的人畜共患脑炎,可以通过及时的暴露后预防 (PEP) 来预防,其中包括伤口护理、狂犬病 和免疫球蛋白的局部浸润。然而,尽管 PEP 的 狂犬病 报告仍引发了人们对 疫苗 功效和方案遵守情况的担忧。这项研究旨在评估这种明显的 狂犬病“突破性”感染的负担和根本原因。方法:本回顾性研究分析了 2018 年至 2024 年接受 PEP 的实验室确诊 狂犬病 病例。包括至少 1 次 狂犬病 疫苗 剂量记录的病例。“真正的”突破性感染(或 PEP 失败)被定义为 狂犬病,尽管在暴露后 2 个日历日内启动了 PEP,并且记录了所有必需 PEP 组件的完成情况,但仍发生了 狂犬病。结果:对符合纳入标准的89例病例进行分析;只有 3 例被归类为“真正的”突破性感染。大多数病例为男性(80.90%),年龄小于15岁(64.04%)。93.26% 的病例报告与狗接触,其中 80.9% 为世界卫生组织 (WHO) III 类接触,34.83% 涉及头部或颈部。57.3% 的病例在 2 天内开始暴露后预防;伤口护理记录为 56.18%,疫苗接种 完成率为 46.07%,只有 26.39% 的 III 类病例接受了 狂犬病 免疫球蛋白,没有任何偏差。结论:研究结果表明,“真正的”突破性感染并不常见,最明显的 PEP 失败是由于医疗服务提供方面的差距造成的。此类失败可能会削弱公众对 疫苗接种 的信心,突显出需要加强医护人员和社区对 PEP 关键步骤的认识。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
狂犬病 是一种致命的人畜共患脑炎,可以通过及时的暴露后预防 (PEP) 来预防,其中包括伤口护理、狂犬病 和免疫球蛋白的局部浸润。然而,尽管 PEP 的 狂犬病 报告仍引发了人们对 疫苗 功效和方案遵守情况的担忧。
设计与对象
这项回顾性研究分析了 2018 年至 2024 年接受暴露后预防的实验室确诊 狂犬病 病例。包括至少 1 次 狂犬病 疫苗 剂量记录的病例。
核心方法
这项研究旨在评估这种明显的 狂犬病“突破性”感染的负担和根本原因。“真正的”突破性感染(或 PEP 失败)被定义为 狂犬病,尽管在暴露后 2 个日历日内启动了 PEP,并且记录了所有必需 PEP 组件的完成情况,但仍发生了 狂犬病。
主要结果
对符合纳入标准的 89 例病例进行了分析;只有 3 例被归类为“真正的”突破性感染。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,“真正的”突破性感染并不常见,最明显的 PEP 失败是由于医疗服务提供方面的差距造成的。
科研与公卫意义
此类失败可能会削弱公众对 疫苗接种 的信心,突显出需要加强医护人员和社区对 PEP 关键步骤的认识。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告研究设计或证据强度评估的具体局限性。

Rabies Despite Postexposure Prophylaxis in High-Burden Settings: Vaccine Failure or Gaps in Healthcare Delivery?

Authors: Lonika Lodha, Shipra Gupta, M A Ashwini, Shubhangi Chandel, Reeta S Mani
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Rabies, a fatal zoonotic encephalitis, is preventable through timely postexposure prophylaxis (PEP), which includes wound care, vaccination, and local infiltration of immunoglobulins. However, reports of rabies despite PEP raise concerns about vaccine efficacy and protocol adherence. This study aimed to assess the burden and underlying causes of such apparent rabies "breakthrough" infections. METHODS: This retrospective study analyzed laboratory-confirmed rabies cases from 2018 to 2024 who had received PEP. Cases with at least 1 documented dose of rabies vaccine were included. "True" breakthrough infections (or PEP failures) were defined as rabies occurring despite initiation of PEP within 2 calendar days of exposure and documented completion of all required PEP components. RESULTS: Eighty-nine cases fulfilling the inclusion criteria were analyzed; only 3 were classified as "true" breakthrough infections. A majority of the cases were males (80.90%), and aged less than 15 years (64.04%). Exposure to dogs was reported in 93.26% cases, 80.9% were World Health Organization (WHO) Category III exposures, and 34.83% involved the head or neck. Postexposure prophylaxis was initiated within 2 days in 57.3% of cases; wound care was documented in 56.18%, vaccination completed in 46.07%, and only 26.39% of Category III cases received rabies immunoglobulins without any deviations. CONCLUSIONS: The findings indicate that "true" breakthrough infections are uncommon, with most apparent PEP failures resulting from gaps in healthcare delivery. Such failures can undermine public confidence in vaccination, highlighting the need to strengthen awareness among healthcare workers and communities about the critical steps of PEP.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Rabies, a fatal zoonotic encephalitis, is preventable through timely postexposure prophylaxis (PEP), which includes wound care, vaccination, and local infiltration of immunoglobulins. However, reports of rabies despite PEP raise concerns about vaccine efficacy and protocol adherence.
Design and population
This retrospective study analyzed laboratory-confirmed rabies cases from 2018 to 2024 who had received postexposure prophylaxis. Cases with at least 1 documented dose of rabies vaccine were included.
Core methods
This study aimed to assess the burden and underlying causes of such apparent rabies "breakthrough" infections. "True" breakthrough infections (or PEP failures) were defined as rabies occurring despite initiation of PEP within 2 calendar days of exposure and documented completion of all required PEP components.
Main results
Eighty-nine cases fulfilling the inclusion criteria were analyzed; only 3 were classified as "true" breakthrough infections.
Interpretation and novelty

Scientific and public-health significance
Such failures can undermine public confidence in vaccination, highlighting the need to strengthen awareness among healthcare workers and communities about the critical steps of PEP.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations of the study design or evidence strength assessment.

加纳上西区劳拉市和吉拉帕市养狗者的 狂犬病 相关知识、态度和做法

Rabies-related knowledge, attitudes, and practices among dog owners in Lawra and Jirapa Municipalities, Upper West Region, Ghana
作者： Alijata Issah Beninmie, Benjamin Tetteh Mensah, Samuel Asumah, Victor Emmanuel Manieson, Amos Sarpong Agyei, Meyir Yiryele Ziekah, Sylvester Languon, Samuel Otis Bel-Nono, Hampton Russell Coombs, Henry Asigri Abugri
摘要中文翻译简介:狂犬病 是一种被忽视的人畜共患疾病,对贫困和偏远人类住区的影响比对城市富裕社区的影响更大。在农村地区,狂犬病 病例常常未被报告,因为人们对该疾病的认识较低,而且 狂犬病 的情况也不佳。对狗生态的了解已被认为是在 狂犬病 流行国家设计有效的 狂犬病 控制计划的关键因素。本研究评估了加纳上西部地区劳拉市和吉拉帕市的狗主人对 狂犬病 控制的知识、态度和实践 (KAP)。方法:通过多阶段抽样,对370名18岁及以上养狗人士进行定量横断面调查。使用结构化问卷收集数据,并使用 Stata 17.0 中的描述性和推论性统计进行分析。生成综合分数以量化 狂犬病 知识、对反 狂犬病 狂犬病 的态度、与狗咬伤相关的做法以及 狂犬病 控制措施。作为研究的一部分,参与家庭的狗免费接种了 狂犬病 疫苗。结果:两个城市的 狂犬病 认知度都很高,吉拉帕为 97.6%,劳拉为 77.0%。疫苗接种 相关实践在 10 个评估领域中得分很高。吉拉帕 (44.1%) 的狗咬伤 患病率 明显高于劳拉 (20.5%)。虽然劳拉 (Lawra) 的大多数咬伤受害者都去了医疗机构,但吉拉帕 (Jirapa) 的 38.4% 的受害者依赖传统疗法。人口因素与 狂犬病 相关 KAP 之间存在关联。结论:该研究建议地方卫生当局、社区领袖和媒体合作参与,将两个城市的高度 狂犬病 意识和积极的 狂犬病 态度转化为加强预防措施,特别是狗咬伤后的适当反应。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
狂犬病 是一种被忽视的人畜共患疾病,对贫困和偏远人类住区的影响比对城市富裕社区的影响更大。本研究评估了加纳上西部地区劳拉市和吉拉帕市的狗主人对 狂犬病 控制的知识、态度和实践 (KAP)。
设计与对象
通过多阶段抽样,对370名18岁及以上的狗主人进行了定量横断面调查。该研究重点关注加纳上西部地区的两个城市,由于认识程度低和 狂犬病 情况不佳,这些城市的 狂犬病 病例经常未被报告。
核心方法
使用结构化问卷收集数据,并使用 Stata 17.0 中的描述性和推论性统计进行分析。生成综合分数以量化 狂犬病 知识、对反 狂犬病 狂犬病 的态度、与狗咬伤相关的做法以及 狂犬病 控制措施。
主要结果
两个城市的 狂犬病 认知度都很高,吉拉帕为 97.6%,劳拉为 77.0%。吉拉帕 (44.1%) 的狗咬伤 患病率 明显高于劳拉 (20.5%)。虽然劳拉 (Lawra) 的大多数咬伤受害者都去了医疗机构,但吉拉帕 (Jirapa) 的 38.4% 的受害者依赖传统疗法。人口因素与 狂犬病 相关 KAP 之间存在关联。
解释与创新
该研究强调,地方卫生当局、社区领袖和媒体需要合作参与,将高度的 狂犬病 意识和积极的 狂犬病 态度转化为加强预防措施,特别是狗咬伤后的适当反应。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以为有针对性的公共卫生干预措施提供信息,以改善农村地区的 狂犬病 预防和应对策略,特别是通过解决对传统疗法的依赖和加强社区参与。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到横截面设计的限制,无法确定因果关系。证据是中等的,因为它提供了对 KAP 的见解,但不包括长期随访或详细的生态数据。

Rabies-related knowledge, attitudes, and practices among dog owners in Lawra and Jirapa Municipalities, Upper West Region, Ghana

Authors: Alijata Issah Beninmie, Benjamin Tetteh Mensah, Samuel Asumah, Victor Emmanuel Manieson, Amos Sarpong Agyei, Meyir Yiryele Ziekah, Sylvester Languon, Samuel Otis Bel-Nono, Hampton Russell Coombs, Henry Asigri Abugri
Original abstract (English)
Introduction: Rabies is a neglected zoonotic disease affecting poor and remote human settlements disproportionately relative to urban rich communities. In rural areas, rabies cases often go unreported, as awareness of the disease is low and surveillance is sub-optimal. An understanding of dog ecology has been recognised as a key factor in designing an effective rabies control program in rabies-endemic countries. This study assessed the knowledge, attitudes, and practices (KAP) of rabies control among dog owners in Lawra and Jirapa Municipalities in the Upper West Region of Ghana. Methods: A quantitative cross-sectional survey was conducted among 370 dog owners aged 18 and above, selected through multistage sampling. Data were collected using structured questionnaires and analysed using descriptive and inferential statistics in Stata 17.0. Composite scores were generated to quantify rabies knowledge, attitudes toward anti-rabies vaccination, dog-bite-related practices, and rabies control measures. As part of the study, dogs in participating households were vaccinated against rabies free of charge. Results: Rabies awareness was high in both municipalities, with Jirapa at 97.6% and Lawra at 77.0%. Vaccination-related practices scored highly across the 10 assessed domains. Dog bite prevalence was notably higher in Jirapa (44.1%) than in Lawra (20.5%). While most bite victims in Lawra visited health facilities, 38.4% in Jirapa relied on traditional remedies. Associations were found between demographic factors and rabies-related KAP. Conclusion: The study recommends collaborative engagement of local health authorities, community leaders, and media to translate the high rabies awareness and positive vaccination attitudes into strengthening preventive practices, particularly appropriate responses following dog bites, in both municipalities.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Rabies is a neglected zoonotic disease affecting poor and remote human settlements disproportionately relative to urban rich communities. This study assessed the knowledge, attitudes, and practices (KAP) of rabies control among dog owners in Lawra and Jirapa Municipalities in the Upper West Region of Ghana.
Design and population
A quantitative cross-sectional survey was conducted among 370 dog owners aged 18 and above, selected through multistage sampling. The study focused on two municipalities in the Upper West Region of Ghana, where rabies cases often go unreported due to low awareness and sub-optimal surveillance.
Core methods
Data were collected using structured questionnaires and analysed using descriptive and inferential statistics in Stata 17.0. Composite scores were generated to quantify rabies knowledge, attitudes toward anti-rabies vaccination, dog-bite-related practices, and rabies control measures.
Main results
Rabies awareness was high in both municipalities, with Jirapa at 97.6% and Lawra at 77.0%. Dog bite prevalence was notably higher in Jirapa (44.1%) than in Lawra (20.5%). While most bite victims in Lawra visited health facilities, 38.4% in Jirapa relied on traditional remedies. Associations were found between demographic factors and rabies-related KAP.
Interpretation and novelty
The study highlights the need for collaborative engagement of local health authorities, community leaders, and media to translate the high rabies awareness and positive vaccination attitudes into strengthening preventive practices, particularly appropriate responses following dog bites.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform targeted public health interventions to improve rabies prevention and response strategies in rural areas, particularly by addressing the reliance on traditional remedies and enhancing community engagement.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its cross-sectional design, which cannot establish causality. The evidence is moderate, as it provides insights into KAP but does not include long-term follow-up or detailed ecological data.

通过人工智能探索人畜共患疾病知识,以增强低资源语言的风险、预防和应对意识

Exploring Zoonotic Disease Knowledge Through AI for Enhanced Risk, Prevention, and Response Awareness in Low-Resource Languages
作者： Reolin Govender, Armand De Wet, Tedson Nkoana, Wanda Markotter, Abiodun Modupe, Vukosi Marivate
摘要中文翻译公共卫生沟通中的语言包容性有限,导致许多南非社区服务不足,特别是有关 狂犬病 等人畜共患疾病的关键信息。这项试点研究通过开发和评估人工智能驱动的方法来解决这一差距,以便向 Sepedi 使用者(一种资源匮乏的语言群体)提供可靠的 狂犬病 信息。该研究提出了一个新颖的、精心策划的 Sepedi 数据集,其中包含 60 个问答对,这些数据集是通过系统管道创建的:对权威英语来源的主题分析指导了 QA 对的合成生成,然后由母语专家进行翻译和手动验证。该数据集用于在基本和微调条件下比较两种大型语言模型 GPT-4o 和 Gemini-1.5 Flash。评估采用以人为本的评估标准,评估流畅性、准确性和文化适宜性。研究结果揭示了将法学硕士应用于资源匮乏领域的一个关键细微差别。基础 GPT-4o 模型具有强大的基础多语言功能,优于所有其他配置,包括其自己的微调变体。相比之下,微调为能力较差的基础 Gemini 模型提供了显着的改进。这个结果表明微调可以增强较弱的模型;它的好处并不普遍,并且在训练数据稀缺时可能会被最先进架构强大的零样本性能所抵消。精心策划的 Sepedi 狂犬病 QA 数据集将在开放许可下发布,以支持未来低资源公共卫生通信方面的工作。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决公共卫生沟通中缺乏语言包容性的问题,特别是有关南非社区 狂犬病 等人畜共患疾病的关键信息。
设计与对象
这项试点研究开发并评估了人工智能驱动的方法,使用精选的问答对数据集向 Sepedi 使用者(一种资源匮乏的语言群体)提供可靠的 狂犬病 信息。
核心方法
该研究创建了一个系统化的流程,涉及对权威英语资源进行主题分析,以生成综合问答对,然后由母语专家进行翻译和手动验证。在基本和微调条件下对两种大型语言模型 GPT-4o 和 Gemini-1.5 Flash 进行了比较。
主要结果
基础 GPT-4o 模型的性能优于所有其他配置,包括其自己的微调变体。微调为能力较差的基本 Gemini 模型提供了显着的改进。
解释与创新
研究结果揭示了将法学硕士应用于低资源领域的一个关键细微差别,表明微调可以增强较弱的模型,但其好处并不普遍,并且在训练数据稀缺时可能会被最先进架构的强大零样本性能所抵消。
科研与公卫意义
精心策划的 Sepedi 狂犬病 QA 数据集将支持未来资源匮乏的公共卫生传播工作,提高健康信息获取有限的社区的风险意识和响应能力。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性在于其重点关注单一语言群体,并且缺乏有关 狂犬病 结果的直接临床或流行病学数据。由于评估的探索性,证据的强度中等。

Exploring Zoonotic Disease Knowledge Through AI for Enhanced Risk, Prevention, and Response Awareness in Low-Resource Languages

Authors: Reolin Govender, Armand De Wet, Tedson Nkoana, Wanda Markotter, Abiodun Modupe, Vukosi Marivate
Original abstract (English)
Limited linguistic inclusivity in public health communication leaves many South African communities underserved, particularly regarding critical information on zoonotic diseases such as rabies. This pilot study addresses this gap by developing and evaluating AI-driven methods for delivering reliable rabies information to Sepedi speakers, a low-resource language group. The study presents a novel, curated Sepedi dataset of 60 question–answer pairs, created through a systematic pipeline: thematic analysis of authoritative English sources guided the synthetic generation of QA pairs, which were then translated and manually verified by a native-speaking expert. This dataset was used to compare two large language models, GPT-4o and Gemini-1.5 Flash, under both base and fine-tuned conditions. Evaluation used a human-centred rubric assessing fluency, accuracy, and cultural appropriateness. The findings reveal a key nuance in applying LLMs to low-resource domains. The base GPT-4o model, with strong foundational multilingual capabilities, outperformed all other configurations, including its own fine-tuned variant.In contrast, fine-tuning provided a marked improvement for the less capable base Gemini model. This result indicates that fine-tuning can enhance weaker models; its benefits are not universal and may be outweighed by the strong zero-shot performance of state-of-the-art architectures when training data is scarce. The curated Sepedi rabies QA dataset will be released under an open licence to support future work in low-resource public health communication.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the lack of linguistic inclusivity in public health communication, particularly for critical information on zoonotic diseases such as rabies in South African communities.
Design and population
This pilot study developed and evaluated AI-driven methods for delivering reliable rabies information to Sepedi speakers, a low-resource language group, using a curated dataset of question–answer pairs.
Core methods
The study created a systematic pipeline involving thematic analysis of authoritative English sources to generate synthetic QA pairs, which were then translated and manually verified by a native-speaking expert. Two large language models, GPT-4o and Gemini-1.5 Flash, were compared under both base and fine-tuned conditions.
Main results
The base GPT-4o model outperformed all other configurations, including its own fine-tuned variant. Fine-tuning provided a marked improvement for the less capable base Gemini model.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings reveal a key nuance in applying LLMs to low-resource domains, indicating that fine-tuning can enhance weaker models, but its benefits are not universal and may be outweighed by the strong zero-shot performance of state-of-the-art architectures when training data is scarce.
Scientific and public-health significance
The curated Sepedi rabies QA dataset will support future work in low-resource public health communication, enhancing risk awareness and response capabilities for communities with limited access to health information.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its focus on a single language group and the absence of direct clinical or epidemiological data on rabies outcomes. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the exploratory nature of the evaluation.

神经回路分析中的跨突触病毒工具:从解剖图谱到功能询问

Transsynaptic viral tools in neural circuit analysis: from anatomical mapping to functional interrogation
作者： Zsolt Boldogkői, Gábor Torma, Zsolt Csabai, Zoltán Máté, Rebeka Fekete, Máté Mizik, Ferenc Erdélyi, Dóra Tombácz, Ádám Dénes
摘要中文翻译跨突触病毒的应用改变了神经回路分析,使神经元连接的映射变得越来越精确。这些方法可大致分为多突触策略和单突触策略。基于伪狂犬病病毒的多突触逆行示踪剂,包括经典选择的 Bartha 毒株和基因工程逆行变种,为啮齿动物的多突触回路组织提供了重要的见解。对于多突触顺行追踪,单纯疱疹病毒 H129 株及其衍生物已被用于绘制输出途径,随后的基因修饰提高了可检测性和实验控制。在过去的几年中,越来越多的注意力转向基于与腺相关病毒辅助系统结合使用的工程 狂犬病病毒 平台的单突触追踪,该平台能够在基因上限制传播到直接的突触前伙伴。病毒追踪越来越多地与功能方法相结合,包括钙和电压成像、光遗传学、化学遗传学和基于重组酶的遗传策略,从而将电路分析从解剖图谱扩展到对定义的神经元群功能的因果询问。展望未来,改进的多突触扩散控制,加上大的转基因容量、追踪分层电路架构的适用性以及合并功能读数的能力,预计将重燃人们对多突触追踪的兴趣。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究通过突触病毒工具解决了神经回路分析的转变,重点关注从解剖映射到神经元连接功能询问的 进化。
设计与对象
该综述将跨突触病毒方法大致分为多突触和单突触策略,检查它们在啮齿动物中的应用以及与功能方法的整合。
核心方法
该分析评估了病毒追踪技术,包括基于伪狂犬病病毒的逆行追踪、单纯疱疹病毒顺行追踪,以及与腺相关病毒辅助系统相结合的工程化 狂犬病病毒 平台,用于单突触追踪。
主要结果
多突触逆行示踪剂(如 Bartha 株伪狂犬病病毒和基因工程变体)为多突触回路组织提供了见解,而单纯疱疹病毒 H129 衍生物已用于顺行作图,并提高了可检测性。
解释与创新
作者解释说,通过与钙成像、光遗传学、化学遗传学和遗传策略的整合,病毒追踪已经从解剖图谱发展到功能询问,从而能够对神经元群体进行因果分析。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现通过展示病毒工具如何与功能方法相结合来了解神经回路的架构和功能,为未来的神经回路研究提供信息,从而有可能推进神经系统疾病的治疗干预。
局限与证据强度
证据强度中等,因为这是一篇总结现有技术的评论文章,而不是提供新的原始数据,其局限性包括需要改进对多突触扩散的控制和多突触追踪的大转基因能力。

Transsynaptic viral tools in neural circuit analysis: from anatomical mapping to functional interrogation

Authors: Zsolt Boldogkői, Gábor Torma, Zsolt Csabai, Zoltán Máté, Rebeka Fekete, Máté Mizik, Ferenc Erdélyi, Dóra Tombácz, Ádám Dénes
Original abstract (English)
The application of transsynaptic viruses has transformed neural circuit analysis, enabling increasingly precise mapping of neuronal connectivity. These approaches can be broadly divided into polysynaptic and monosynaptic strategies. Polysynaptic retrograde tracers based on pseudorabies virus, including the classically selected Bartha strain and genetically engineered retrograde variants have provided important insights into multisynaptic circuit organization in rodents. For polysynaptic anterograde tracing, herpes simplex virus strain H129 and its derivatives have been used to map output pathways, with later genetic modifications improving detectability and experimental control. Over the past few years, growing attention has shifted toward monosynaptic tracing based on engineered rabies virus platforms used in combination with adeno-associated virus helper systems, which enable genetically restricted spread to direct presynaptic partners. Viral tracing is increasingly integrated with functional approaches, including calcium and voltage imaging, optogenetics, chemogenetics, and recombinase-based genetic strategies, thereby extending circuit analysis from anatomical mapping to causal interrogation of the function of defined neuronal populations. Looking forward, improved control of polysynaptic spread, together with large transgene capacity, suitability for tracing hierarchical circuit architecture, and the ability to incorporate functional readouts, is expected to revive interest in multisynaptic tracing.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the transformation of neural circuit analysis through transsynaptic viral tools, focusing on their evolution from anatomical mapping to functional interrogation of neuronal connectivity.
Design and population
The review broadly categorizes transsynaptic viral approaches into polysynaptic and monosynaptic strategies, examining their applications in rodents and integration with functional methods.
Core methods
The analysis evaluates viral tracing techniques including pseudorabies virus-based retrograde tracers, herpes simplex virus anterograde tracing, and engineered rabies virus platforms combined with adeno-associated virus helper systems for monosynaptic tracing.
Main results
Polysynaptic retrograde tracers like Bartha strain pseudorabies virus and genetically engineered variants have provided insights into multisynaptic circuit organization, while herpes simplex virus H129 derivatives have been used for anterograde mapping with improved detectability.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that viral tracing has evolved from anatomical mapping to functional interrogation through integration with calcium imaging, optogenetics, chemogenetics, and genetic strategies, enabling causal analysis of neuronal populations.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform future neural circuit research by demonstrating how viral tools can be combined with functional approaches to understand circuit architecture and function, potentially advancing therapeutic interventions for neurological disorders.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate as this is a review article summarizing existing techniques rather than presenting new primary data, with limitations including the need for improved control of polysynaptic spread and large transgene capacity for multisynaptic tracing.

具有部分耦合扩散的反应扩散系统的行波

Traveling waves of a reaction-diffusion system with partially coupled diffusion
作者： Thomas Giletti, Hirofumi Izuhara, Harunori Monobe
摘要中文翻译这项工作致力于研究反应扩散系统的行波解,其中第二个分量 ~$v$ 的扩散速率取决于第一个分量 ~$u$。这样的系统出现在猎物-捕食者模型中,其中捕食者〜$v$正在积极捕猎其猎物〜$u$,或者出现在疾病引起不稳定行为的流行病学模型中,例如狂犬病。这导致 ~$v$ 方程的最高阶项出现拟线性耦合。从理论角度来看,当第一分量不扩散时,我们对行波解进行完全分类,在这种情况下,问题可以简化为非线性标量方程。对两种成分扩散的情况进行了数值研究。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在对扩散速率部分耦合的反应扩散系统中的行波解进行分类,并将其应用于 狂犬病 等流行病学模型。
设计与对象
该研究的重点是反应扩散系统中行波解的理论分类和数值研究,特别是当第一组分不扩散时。
核心方法
当第一个分量不扩散时,理论分析将问题简化为非线性标量方程,而对于两个分量都扩散的情况,则使用数值方法。
主要结果
当第一个分量不扩散时,行波解被完全分类,并且对两个分量都扩散的情况进行了数值研究。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为理解部分耦合系统中行波解的理论进步,并且在特定扩散条件下的解分类方面具有新颖性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为疾病传播的数学模型提供了信息,特别是对于 狂犬病,其不稳定的行为会影响扩散率,有助于流行病预测和控制策略。
局限与证据强度
由于依赖理论和数值方法而没有实证验证,证据强度中等,并且研究范围仅限于特定的扩散场景。

Traveling waves of a reaction-diffusion system with partially coupled diffusion

Authors: Thomas Giletti, Hirofumi Izuhara, Harunori Monobe
Original abstract (English)
This work is devoted to the study of traveling wave solutions of reaction-diffusion systems, where the diffusion rate of~$v$, the second component, depends on~$u$, the first component. Such systems arise in prey-predator models, where the predator~$v$ is actively hunting its prey~$u$, or in epidemiological models where the disease induces erratic behavior, for example rabies. This results in a quasilinear coupling in the highest-order term of the equation for~$v$. From the theoretical point of view, we fully classify traveling wave solutions when the first component does not diffuse, in which case the problem can be reduced to a nonlinear scalar equation. The case where both components diffuse is investigated numerically.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to classify traveling wave solutions in reaction-diffusion systems where diffusion rates are partially coupled, with applications to epidemiological models like rabies.
Design and population
The research focused on theoretical classification and numerical investigation of traveling wave solutions in reaction-diffusion systems, particularly when the first component does not diffuse.
Core methods
Theoretical analysis reduced the problem to a nonlinear scalar equation when the first component does not diffuse, while numerical methods were used for cases where both components diffuse.
Main results
Traveling wave solutions were fully classified when the first component does not diffuse, and the case where both components diffuse was investigated numerically.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as a theoretical advancement in understanding traveling wave solutions in partially coupled systems, with novelty in the classification of solutions under specific diffusion conditions.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform mathematical modeling of disease spread, particularly for rabies, where erratic behavior affects diffusion rates, aiding in epidemic prediction and control strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate due to the reliance on theoretical and numerical approaches without empirical validation, and the study's scope is limited to specific diffusion scenarios.

📗 主报告：综述 / Primary Reviews

Journal: Jaffna Medical Journal  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-07-21  |  DOI: 10.4038/jmj.v38i1.257  |  Date basis: online_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 38, 1, 18-25
A 高优先级学术文献 · 综述A high priorityAcademic literature · Review

贾夫纳区的人类 狂犬病 狂犬病:是否是由于 COVID-19 对 狂犬病 控制活动的影响?

Human Rabies Outbreak in Jaffna District: Is it due to the impact of COVID-19 on rabies control activities?
作者： S. Sivaganesh, S. Suganja
摘要中文翻译尽管斯里兰卡的 狂犬病 死亡人数在 COVID 19 期间/之后没有显着变化,但贾夫纳的病例在此期间显着增加。本研究旨在确定斯里兰卡贾夫纳区 狂犬病 死亡人数增加的可能因素。通过从贾夫纳地区卫生服务局和贾夫纳区卫生办公室医疗官员保存的登记册/记录中收集 2019-2022 年的数据,进行了案头审查。使用 Microsoft Excel 中从通知登记册、电子邮件通知、特殊 监测 表格、狗免疫登记册/记录、狗绝育登记册/记录以及医院每月申报表中准备的数据提取表收集数据。对数据进行三角测量以确保数据质量。使用 Microsoft Excel 函数对收集的数据进行分析。使用简单的描述性统计来描述分布和比较。在 14 例 狂犬病 死亡中,大多数病例 (93%) 发生在从事经济活动的成年人中,(64%) 发生在男性中。病例分布主要在两个地区,即瓦利卡马姆和瓦达马拉德奇。MOH Sandilipay 的 发病率 最高(36%)。OPD 服务员中被动物咬伤的比例从 2019 年的 6.9% 到 2020 年的 11.1% 和 2021 年的 17.6%。与 2021 年相比,2022 年有所减少。多年来,狗绝育的数量一直在波动。该地区的年度狗 疫苗接种 覆盖率从 2019 年的 29.3% 到 2022 年的 86.2%。2019 年,该地区的覆盖率从 MOH Chankanai、MOH Karainagar、MOH Kayts 和 MOH Kopay 的 0% 到 MOH Chavakacheri 的 105.5%。同样,到 2022 年,这一比例从贾夫纳卫生部的 46.3% 到塞利帕莱卫生部的 106.6%。据观察,多年来,随着狗 疫苗接种 覆盖率的增加,人类 狂犬病 死亡人数有所减少。有足够的证据表明,在 COVID-19 期间,狂犬病 控制活动有所减少,导致贾夫纳区动物咬伤和 狂犬病 病例数量较高。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本综述研究了斯里兰卡贾夫纳区的人类 狂犬病 狂犬病,重点关注 COVID-19 是否影响了 狂犬病 控制活动。核心问题是确定导致大流行期间 狂犬病 死亡人数增加的因素。
证据基础与方法
使用从贾夫纳地区卫生服务局和卫生官员办公室收集的 2019-2022 年数据进行了案头审查。使用 Microsoft Excel 从通知登记册、电子邮件通知、监测 表格、狗免疫/绝育记录和医院每月报表中提取数据。三角测量确保了数据质量,并使用描述性统计进行分析。
共识与结论
有强有力的证据表明,在 COVID-19 期间,狂犬病 控制活动减少,导致贾夫纳的动物咬伤和 狂犬病 病例增加。狗 疫苗接种 覆盖率的增加与人类 狂犬病 死亡人数的减少相关。大多数 狂犬病 死亡 (93%) 发生在从事经济活动的成年人中,其中 64% 是男性,病例集中在瓦利卡马姆和瓦达马拉奇地区。
争议与缺口
审查发现了卫生官员地区狗绝育率的波动和 疫苗接种 覆盖率不一致(范围从 0% 到 106.6%)。尽管该研究表明 COVID-19 扰乱了 狂犬病 的控制,但它并未充分探讨 2022 年之后的其他潜在影响因素或长期影响。
科研与实践启示
优先事项包括加强 狂犬病 狂犬病 系统、提高所有地区狗 狂犬病 的一致覆盖率,以及制定突发公共卫生事件期间维持 狂犬病 控制的应急计划。建议在 MOH Sandilipay 等高 发病率 地区采取强化数据收集方法和有针对性的干预措施。

Human Rabies Outbreak in Jaffna District: Is it due to the impact of COVID-19 on rabies control activities?

Authors: S. Sivaganesh, S. Suganja
Original abstract (English)
Though the number of rabies deaths in Sri Lanka has not changed significantly during/post COVID 19 period, cases in Jaffna has shown a significant increase during this period. This study aimed to determine the possible factors for the increased rabies deaths in the Jaffna District, Sri Lanka.A Desk review was conducted by collecting data of years 2019-2022 from the registers/records maintained in Regional Directorate of Health Services, Jaffna and Medical Officer of Health offices in the Jaffna District. Data was collected using data extraction sheets prepared in Microsoft excel from notification registers, e-mail notifications, special surveillance forms, dog immunization registers/records, dog sterilization registers/records and monthly returns from the hospitals. Triangulation of data was done to ensure data quality. Collected data was analyzed using Microsoft excel functions. Simple descriptive statistics was used to describe the distribution and comparison.Out of 14 rabies death, most of the cases (93%) have occurred among economically active adults and (64%) among males. Distribution of cases was mainly in two land masses, namely Valikamam and Vadamaradchi. MOH Sandilipay had the highest incidence (36%). Proportion of animal bites among OPD attendants varied from 6.9% in 2019 to 11.1% in 2020 and 17.6% in 2021.A reduction was observed in 2022 compared to 2021. Number of dog sterilizations has been fluctuating over the years. Annual dog vaccination coverage in the district varied from 29.3% in 2019 to 86.2% in 2022. In 2019, it varied from 0% in MOH Chankanai, MOH Karainagar, MOH Kayts and MOH Kopay to 105.5% in MOH Chavakacheri. Similarly, in 2022, it varied from 46.3% in MOH Jaffna to 106.6% in MOH Thellipalai. It has observed reduction in human rabies death following increased dog vaccination coverage over the years. There is enough evidence that rabies control activities had been reduced during COVID-19 period and resulted high incidence of animal bites and rabies cases in Jaffna District.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This review examines the human rabies outbreak in Jaffna District, Sri Lanka, focusing on whether COVID-19 impacted rabies control activities. The central question is to determine factors contributing to increased rabies deaths during the pandemic period.
Evidence base and method
A desk review was conducted using data from 2019-2022 collected from Regional Directorate of Health Services and Medical Officer of Health offices in Jaffna. Data was extracted from notification registers, email notifications, surveillance forms, dog immunization/sterilization records, and hospital monthly returns using Microsoft Excel. Triangulation ensured data quality, and descriptive statistics were used for analysis.
Consensus and conclusions
There is strong evidence that rabies control activities were reduced during COVID-19, leading to increased animal bites and rabies cases in Jaffna. Increased dog vaccination coverage correlated with reduced human rabies deaths. Most rabies deaths (93%) occurred among economically active adults, with 64% among males, and cases were concentrated in Valikamam and Vadamaradchi regions.
Controversies and gaps
The review identifies fluctuations in dog sterilization rates and inconsistent vaccination coverage across Medical Officer of Health areas (ranging from 0% to 106.6%). While the study suggests COVID-19 disrupted rabies control, it does not fully explore other potential contributing factors or long-term impacts beyond 2022.
Research and practice implications
Priorities include strengthening rabies surveillance systems, improving consistent dog vaccination coverage across all regions, and developing contingency plans for maintaining rabies control during public health emergencies. Enhanced data collection methods and targeted interventions in high-incidence areas like MOH Sandilipay are recommended.

通过单克隆抗体疗法克服 狂犬病 免疫逃避。

Overcoming rabies immune evasion via monoclonal antibody therapeutics.
作者： Md Jashim Uddin, Jocelyn King, Christopher C Broder, Brian C Schaefer
摘要中文翻译狂犬病 是一种致命的急性病毒性脑炎,由嗜神经性狂犬病病毒属病毒(特别是狂犬病病毒属 狂犬病)引起。目前针对 狂犬病 暴露的 治疗 涉及 暴露后预防 (PEP),其中包括 狂犬病、疫苗 和 狂犬病 免疫球蛋白 (RIG) 的施用。不存在有效的 治疗 来治愈有症状的 狂犬病。由于 PEP 的可用性有限且成本高昂,全世界每年仍然有数万人死亡。单克隆抗体 (mAb) 是 RIG 的有效且经济的替代品。此外,最近的临床前研究提供的证据表明单克隆抗体可以有效治疗有症状的 狂犬病,克服病毒免疫逃避机制。在这篇综述中,我们批判性地总结了最近的研究,重点关注使用单克隆抗体的 狂犬病 暴露的 治疗 以及基于单克隆抗体的疗法对抗症状性 狂犬病 的潜力。

Overcoming rabies immune evasion via monoclonal antibody therapeutics.

Authors: Md Jashim Uddin, Jocelyn King, Christopher C Broder, Brian C Schaefer
Original abstract (English)
Rabies is a lethal acute viral encephalitis caused by neurotropic lyssaviruses, particularly Lyssavirus rabies. The current treatment for rabies exposure involves post-exposure prophylaxis (PEP), which includes the administration of the rabies vaccine and rabies immunoglobulins (RIGs). No effective treatments exist to cure symptomatic rabies. Due to the limited availability and high cost of PEP, tens of thousands of deaths still occur worldwide every year. Monoclonal antibodies (mAbs) represent an effective and economical alternative to RIGs. Moreover, recent preclinical studies have provided evidence that monoclonal antibodies can effectively treat symptomatic rabies, overcoming mechanisms of viral immune evasion. In this review, we critically summarize recent studies, focusing on the treatment of rabies exposures using monoclonal antibodies and the potential of monoclonal antibody-based therapies to combat symptomatic rabies.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Rabies is a lethal acute viral encephalitis caused by neurotropic lyssaviruses, particularly Lyssavirus rabies. No effective treatments exist to cure symptomatic rabies.
Evidence base and method
The current treatment for rabies exposure involves post-exposure prophylaxis (PEP), which includes the administration of the rabies vaccine and rabies immunoglobulins (RIGs).
Consensus and conclusions
Monoclonal antibodies (mAbs) represent an effective and economical alternative to RIGs. Moreover, recent preclinical studies have provided evidence that monoclonal antibodies can effectively treat symptomatic rabies, overcoming mechanisms of viral immune evasion.
Controversies and gaps
Due to the limited availability and high cost of PEP, tens of thousands of deaths still occur worldwide every year.
Research and practice implications
In this review, we critically summarize recent studies, focusing on the treatment of rabies exposures using monoclonal antibodies and the potential of monoclonal antibody-based therapies to combat symptomatic rabies.

印度医生对 狂犬病 暴露后预防(包括 狂犬病 单克隆抗体)的知识、态度和实践的多州、横断面回顾性研究

A Multi-State, Cross-Sectional Retrospective Study on Knowledge, Attitude and Practice of Rabies Post-Exposure Prophylaxis Including Rabies Monoclonal Antibody Among Physicians Across India
作者： Ravish Haradanahalli Shankaraiah, Navyashree N. S., Divya Bharathi G., Trayambak Dutta, Manish Mahajan, D. G
摘要中文翻译背景/目标:狂犬病 在印度仍然是一种致命的 疫苗 可预防疾病,处理动物咬伤病例的医生对于有效 暴露后预防 (PEP) 至关重要。随着越来越多地使用 狂犬病 单克隆抗体 (RmAb) 作为传统 狂犬病 免疫球蛋白的替代品,本研究评估了医生关于 狂犬病 PEP 的知识、态度和实践 (KAP),包括 RmAb 认知和接受度。方法:这项多州、横断面回顾性 KAP 调查是使用结构化、自我管理的 Google 表格对印度 10 个州的 208 名医生进行的。这份包含 33 项的问卷评估了知识、态度和实践领域,每个领域 11 个项目,根据 NCDC 指南进行二进制评分。进行了描述性统计、Shapiro-Wilk 正态性检验、带有事后修正的 Kruskal-Wallis 检验以及 Spearman 排序相关性。结果:知识领域平均得分为 8.00/11 (72.7%),态度领域得分为 7.22/11 (65.6%),实践领域得分为 8.64/11 (78.5%)。在肌内暴露前预防方案、伤口浸润后过量 RIG/RmAb 的管理、动物接触的暴露分类以及幼儿 疫苗 给药部位等方面发现了关键差距。知识与实践的相关性中等强且具有统计显着性(r = 0.520,p < 0.001)。RmAb 的接受度很高,88.5% 的人认为它们是 RIG 的有效替代品,84.6% 的人认为它们比 RIG 更安全。与 2013 年基准调查相比,实践分数显示出最一致的改善,大多数项目的知识分数有所提高,但暴露前预防计划的知识分数急剧下降,态度分数显示出得与失的混合模式。结论:KAP 总体得分足够,其中实践得分最高。管理 狂犬病 暴露的医生需要开展有针对性的继续医学教育,重点关注 NCDC 指南和实际 PEP 场景。

范围与问题

A Multi-State, Cross-Sectional Retrospective Study on Knowledge, Attitude and Practice of Rabies Post-Exposure Prophylaxis Including Rabies Monoclonal Antibody Among Physicians Across India

Authors: Ravish Haradanahalli Shankaraiah, Navyashree N. S., Divya Bharathi G., Trayambak Dutta, Manish Mahajan, D. G
Original abstract (English)
Background/Objectives: Rabies remains a lethal vaccine-preventable disease in India, and physicians managing animal-bite cases are central to effective post-exposure prophylaxis (PEP). With the increasing use of rabies monoclonal antibodies (RmAbs) as alternatives to conventional rabies immunoglobulins, this study assessed physicians’ knowledge, attitude, and practice (KAP) regarding rabies PEP, including RmAb awareness and acceptance. Methods: This multi-state, cross-sectional retrospective KAP survey was conducted among 208 physicians across ten Indian states using a structured, self-administered Google Form. The 33-item questionnaire assessed Knowledge, Attitude, and Practice domains, with 11 items each, scored binarily according to NCDC guidelines. Descriptive statistics, Shapiro–Wilk normality testing, Kruskal–Wallis tests with post hoc corrections, and Spearman rank-order correlations were performed. Results: Mean domain scores were 8.00/11 for Knowledge (72.7%), 7.22/11 for Attitude (65.6%), and 8.64/11 for Practice (78.5%). Key gaps were identified in the intramuscular pre-exposure prophylaxis schedule, management of excess RIG/RmAb after wound infiltration, exposure categorization of animal contact, and vaccine administration site in young children. The Knowledge–Practice correlation was moderately strong and statistically significant (r = 0.520, p < 0.001). Acceptance of RmAbs was high, with 88.5% recognizing them as effective replacements for RIGs and 84.6% considering them safer than RIGs. Practice scores showed the most consistent improvement, Knowledge scores improved on most items but declined sharply for the pre-exposure prophylaxis schedule, and Attitude scores showed a mixed pattern of gains and losses compared with the 2013 benchmark survey. Conclusions: Overall KAP scores were adequate, with Practice scoring highest. Targeted continuing medical education focused on NCDC guidelines and practical PEP scenarios is needed among physicians managing rabies exposures.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Background/Objectives: Rabies remains a lethal vaccine-preventable disease in India, and physicians managing animal-bite cases are central to effective post-exposure prophylaxis (PEP).
Evidence base and method
Review type: Cross-sectional retrospective KAP survey; Databases: Not specified; Search dates: Not applicable; Eligibility criteria: Physicians across ten Indian states; Selection method: Self-administered Google Form; Included-study count: 208 physicians; Meta-analysis or consensus process: Not reported.
Consensus and conclusions
Major areas of agreement: Overall KAP scores were adequate, with Practice scoring highest (78.5%); Acceptance of RmAbs was high (88.5% recognized effectiveness, 84.6% considered safer than RIGs); Knowledge–Practice correlation was moderately strong (r = 0.520, p < 0.001).
Controversies and gaps
Heterogeneity: Mixed pattern of gains and losses in Attitude scores compared to 2013 benchmark; Key gaps: Intramuscular pre-exposure prophylaxis schedule, management of excess RIG/RmAb, exposure categorization, vaccine administration site in young children; Weak methods: Reliance on self-reported data; Missing populations: Physicians not included in the survey.
Research and practice implications
Targeted continuing medical education focused on NCDC guidelines and practical PEP scenarios is needed among physicians managing rabies exposures.

麻痹性狂犬病脑脊膜炎:附影像学表现及相关文献复习二例

Paralytic Rabies Encephalomeningomyelitis: A Tale of Two Cases With Emphasis on Imaging Findings and Relevant Literature Review
作者： Ketki Kedar, Ami V Patel, Chetana Ratnaparkhi, Avinash Dhok, Sunita Kumbhalkar
摘要中文翻译由于该病的进展性和致命性,狂犬病 脑炎的影像学记录仍然很少见。我们介绍了两例麻痹性 狂犬病 脑脊膜脊髓炎病例,显示出不同的影像学表现,其中一例为一名 2 岁女孩,其磁共振成像 (MRI) 显示出典型特征,另一例为一名 65 岁女性,其 MRI 表现不典型。这两个病例强调,经典的 狂犬病 显示主要灰质受累,而非典型表现,特别是在患有潜在合并症的老年患者中,与典型形式相反,显示出对白质的偏好。识别这种不同的成像光谱对于及时 诊断 至关重要,尤其是在流行地区。狂犬病 生前神经影像学的罕见性,以及典型和非典型病例的详细记录,丰富了有限的诊断文献,并提高了对该疾病影像谱的早期识别。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述的重点是 狂犬病病毒,特别是 狂犬病 脑炎和麻痹性 狂犬病 脑脊膜脊髓炎,重点是人类病例的影像学表现。核心问题涉及 狂犬病 的影像特征谱,特别是比较不同年龄组的典型和非典型表现。
证据基础与方法
该综述介绍了两例 病例报告 麻痹性 狂犬病 脑脊膜脊髓炎,并附有详细的 MRI 文件。没有描述系统搜索或荟萃分析;证据基础由这两个案例组成,并辅以文献综述。
共识与结论
经典的 狂犬病 主要涉及灰质,而不典型的表现,特别是在患有合并症的老年患者中,显示出对白质的偏好。识别这种不同的成像光谱对于流行区的及时 诊断 至关重要。
争议与缺口
狂犬病 生前神经影像学的罕见性限制了诊断文献,典型和非典型病例的详细记录都很少。这一差距阻碍了对该疾病的完整成像谱的早期识别。
科研与实践启示
需要改进 狂犬病 成像结果的记录,特别是非典型病例,以丰富有限的诊断文献并增强早期识别。这在流行区尤其重要,因为及时 诊断 至关重要。

Paralytic Rabies Encephalomeningomyelitis: A Tale of Two Cases With Emphasis on Imaging Findings and Relevant Literature Review

Authors: Ketki Kedar, Ami V Patel, Chetana Ratnaparkhi, Avinash Dhok, Sunita Kumbhalkar
Original abstract (English)
Imaging documentation of rabies encephalitis remains rare because of the progressive and fatal course of the disease. We present two cases of paralytic rabies encephalomeningomyelitis, showing varied imaging findings, one in a two-year-old girl whose magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) illustrated typical features, and another case of a 65-year-old female with atypical MRI findings. These two cases emphasize that classical rabies shows predominant grey-matter involvement, whereas an atypical presentation, particularly in elderly patients with underlying comorbidities, shows a predilection for white matter, in contrast to the typical form. The identification of this varied imaging spectrum is crucial for prompt diagnosis, especially in endemic zones. The rarity of antemortem neuroimaging in rabies, with detailed documentation of typical and atypical cases, enriches the limited diagnostic literature and improves early recognition of the disease’s imaging spectrum.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on rabies virus, specifically rabies encephalitis and paralytic rabies encephalomeningomyelitis, with emphasis on imaging findings in human cases. The central question concerns the spectrum of imaging features in rabies, particularly comparing typical and atypical presentations across different age groups.
Evidence base and method
The review presents two case reports of paralytic rabies encephalomeningomyelitis, with detailed MRI documentation. No systematic search or meta-analysis is described; the evidence base consists of these two cases supplemented by literature review.
Consensus and conclusions
Classical rabies predominantly involves grey matter, while atypical presentations, especially in elderly patients with comorbidities, show a predilection for white matter. The identification of this varied imaging spectrum is crucial for prompt diagnosis in endemic zones.
Controversies and gaps
The rarity of antemortem neuroimaging in rabies limits the diagnostic literature, with detailed documentation of both typical and atypical cases being scarce. This gap hinders early recognition of the disease's full imaging spectrum.
Research and practice implications
Improved documentation of rabies imaging findings, particularly atypical cases, is needed to enrich the limited diagnostic literature and enhance early recognition. This is especially important in endemic zones where prompt diagnosis can be critical.

通过过敏原监测增强 狂犬病 疫苗 安全性:用于量化 β-丙内酯灭活后纯化散装材料中残留 HSA 的时间分辨荧光免疫分析

Enhancing rabies vaccine safety through allergen monitoring: A time-resolved fluoroimmunoassay for quantifying residual HSA in purified bulk material after β-propiolactone inactivation

巴西捐赠10,000剂人用狂犬病 疫苗

Donation from Brazil of 10,000 doses of human rabies vaccine
新闻简报2026 年 7 月 22 日,在泛美卫生组织、泛美卫生组织、美国疾病预防控制中心和粮食计划署的协助下,海地公共卫生部收到了巴西政府捐赠的 10,000 剂人用 狂犬病 疫苗。这笔捐款的发放正值海地局势令人担忧,2026 年前 24 周内报告了 1,481 起疑似动物袭击事件和 3 人死亡事件,主要发生在中部和西部省份。疫苗 将加强海地的库存,从而更好地开展 疫苗 暴露后和预防工作。这一举措强调了区域团结和国际合作,以应对海地持续存在的 狂犬病 威胁,这种疾病一旦出现症状几乎总是致命的,但通过及时干预是可以预防的。

时间
发表日期:2026-07-25。事件日期:2026年7月22日,星期四。

海地,具体是中部和西部部门。受影响人群:被动物咬伤的人。

已确认巴西政府通过泛美卫生组织向海地公共卫生部捐赠了 10,000 剂人用 狂犬病 疫苗。

疫苗 在 PROMESS 仓库收到。在帕纳夫托萨 (PANAFTOSA) 的资助下,美国卫生当局将加强库存,以改善护理和预防。MSPP 与国际合作伙伴持续努力减少与 狂犬病 相关的死亡。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Donation from Brazil of 10,000 doses of human rabies vaccine

News Brief
On July 22, 2026, Haiti's Ministry of Public Health received a significant donation of 10,000 doses of human rabies vaccine from the Brazilian Government, facilitated by PAHO, PANAFTOSA, the U.S. CDC, and WFP. This donation arrives amid a concerning rabies situation in Haiti, where 1,481 suspected animal attacks and 3 deaths were reported in the first 24 weeks of 2026, primarily in the Central and Western departments. The vaccines will bolster Haiti's stockpile, enabling better post-exposure treatment and prevention efforts. This initiative highlights regional solidarity and international cooperation in addressing Haiti's persistent rabies threat, a disease that is nearly always fatal once symptoms appear but preventable with timely intervention.
Original source text (English)

Haiti - Maritime Scholarships : Call for Applications Haiti - Security : The United States lifts restrictions that limited its cooperation with the FAd'H Haiti - Moscow : Haitian student Slanda François, detained in Russia, released Zapping Haiti of July 24, 2026 more news Donation from Brazil of 10,000 doses of human rabies vaccine Haiti's PM pays tribute to the great figures of Konpa FLASH : Medinel Zamor injured, out of the U-20 Champions League Championship Colombia : Haiti's Mission on cocoa Venezuela : Haiti honors its medical brigade returning from its solidarity mission more news Donation from Brazil of 10,000 doses of human rabies vaccine 25/07/2026 11:02:52 On Thursday, July 22, 2026, the Ministry of Public Health received a donation of 10,000 doses of human rabies vaccine at the PROMESS warehouse. The donation was provided by the Government of Brazil through the Pan American Health Organization (PAHO). The delivery of this significant donation to Haiti was made possible through the joint efforts of PANAFTOSA, the U.S. Centers for Disease Control and Prevention (CDC), and the logistical support of the World Food Programme (WFP), which facilitated the air transport of the vaccines to Port-au-Prince. This donation comes at a time when rabies remains a public health threat in Haiti. Between the first and twenty-fourth epidemiological weeks of 2026, 1,481 attacks by animals suspected of having rabies were reported in the country, including 3 deaths, with the situation remaining concentrated in the Central and Western departments. Although this disease is preventable with prompt treatment after exposure, it is almost always fatal once symptoms appear. Access to rabies vaccines is therefore essential to protect populations exposed to animal bites. Thanks to this contribution from the Brazilian Government, health authorities will have a strengthened stockpile, enabling them to improve the care of people exposed to rabies and support prevention efforts in several departments of the country. This initiative illustrates the importance of regional solidarity and coordinated international cooperation in addressing health challenges. It is part of the ongoing efforts of the Ministry of Public Health and Population (MSPP), with the support of friendly countries like Brazil and the United States, and its partners like the Pan American Health Organization (PAHO), to reduce the risk of rabies-related deaths and ensure better access to essential health products. 狂犬病 — 如何保护您的宠物和亲人免受病毒感染

Rabies — how to protect your pets and loved ones from the virus
新闻简报南卡罗来纳州卫生官员已确认查尔斯顿县、约克县和萨鲁达县出现三起 狂犬病 病例,涉及两只患狂犬病的浣熊和一只患狂犬病的蝙蝠。这些病例正在根据健康指南接受治疗或隔离。病毒通过接触受感染动物的唾液(例如咬伤或抓伤)传播。今年报告了 62 起动物狂犬病病例(平均每年 136 起),官员们注意到 8 月至 10 月期间呈典型增长趋势。为了防止接触,居民应避免接触野生动物,确保宠物接种疫苗,如果接触,请立即就医。24 小时报告热线和额外资源可用于事件报告和野生动物清除。

发布于 2026 年 7 月 21 日。未指定事件日期,但最近确认了病例。

南卡罗来纳州,特别是查尔斯顿县、约克县和萨鲁达县。受影响的人群包括人类和宠物。

B routine priorityPublic-health news

Rabies — how to protect your pets and loved ones from the virus

News Brief
South Carolina health officials have confirmed three rabies cases in Charleston, York, and Saluda Counties, involving two rabid raccoons and one rabid bat. These cases are being treated or quarantined per health guidelines. The virus spreads through contact with infected animal saliva, such as bites or scratches. With 62 rabid animal cases reported this year—averaging 136 annually—officials note a typical increase between August and October. To prevent exposure, residents should avoid handling wild animals, ensure pets are vaccinated, and seek immediate medical attention if exposed. A 24-hour reporting line and additional resources are available for incident reporting and wildlife removal.
Original source text (English)
SectionsNews Watch Live Local News National News Politics Entertainment News Watch Live Sports Bahakel Sports Local Sports National Sports USC Gamecocks Clemson Gamecock Saturday Night High School Football –Friday Night Fever Weather Today’s Forecast Radar Weather Photo of the Week Weather Blog Programming ABC DABL Heroes & Icons MeTV MeTV Toons Start TV TV Schedule Children’s Programming Mugshots SecondaryTraffic Contests Tyler’s Travels, Love it or Chuck it Midlands Cares Midlands Military Matters Midlands Pup Shots America 250 WOLOMeTV About Us Contact Us Local Events ABC Columbia Search MeTV About Us Contact Us Local Events News Watch Live Local News National News Politics Entertainment News Watch Live Sports Bahakel Sports Local Sports National Sports USC Gamecocks Clemson Gamecock Saturday Night High School Football –Friday Night Fever Weather Today’s Forecast Radar Weather Photo of the Week Weather Blog Programming ABC DABL Heroes & Icons MeTV MeTV Toons Start TV TV Schedule Children’s Programming Mugshots Traffic Contests Tyler’s Travels, Love it or Chuck it Midlands Cares Midlands Military Matters Midlands Pup Shots America 250 Rabies — how to protect your pets and loved ones from the virus Jul 21, 2026 5:50 PM EDT Lee Williams, COLUMBIA, SC (WOLO) — Health officials with the South Carolina Department of Public Health confirmed three cases of rabies -two rabid racoons and one rabid bat across Charleston, York, and Saluda Counties. All are being treated or quarantined according to health guidelines. So how is rabies contracted? Health officials say the virus can be transmitted through contact with an infected animal’s saliva, such as from a bite or scratch. According to the CDC, animals most frequently found with rabies in the U.S. are bats, skunks, raccoons, and foxes. Around 100,000 people across the U.S. are vaccinated against the virus following a potential rabies exposure each year. DPH’s Rabies Program Manager, Terri McCollister, says there are ways to try and steer clear from the virus. First — never handle a bat or any wild or stray animal with your bare hands, whether it’s dead or alive. When it comes to bats, McCollister says people don’t always realize that they, or a pet, have been bitten — since the animal’s teeth are so tiny — making bites easy to overlook. Because of this, you should always assume a person, or pet, has potentially been bitten should you wake up to find a bat in, say, a bedroom or tent — or a bat is found where children, pets, or mentally impaired people have been left unattended. According to McCollister, a bat that is active by day, found where bats are not usually seen, or is unable to fly is far more likely to be rabid. However, there is no guarantee that a rabid bat will behave any differently than a healthy bat. If you think you may have been exposed to an animal with rabies, immediately wash the affected area with plenty of soap and water, and seek medical attention to determine whether any follow‐up care is needed. If you choose not to seek medical care, contact your county office to report the incident so they can try and track down the animal. To help protect pets, McCollister says keep them up-to-date on their rabies vaccination — which remains one of the most effective defenses. DPH reports 62 cases of rabid animals so far this year. On average, about 136 are reported annually. McCollister also says it is a normal trend to see increased positive cases of rabies in South Carolina between the months of August and October. The exact reason for this isn’t known, but there are several human and animal behavior factors that might be involved. Other southeastern states have similar trends. If you believe you, someone you know, or a pet has been exposed, you can call DPH’s 24-hour rabies reporting line at (888) 847-0902 (select option 2). You can also find additional reporting information on DPH’s website here. For a list of wildlife animal removal services, click here. Response, status and uncertainty

2026 年上半年越南记录 39 例 狂犬病 死亡 - DTiNews

Vietnam records 39 rabies deaths in first half of 2026 - DTiNews
新闻简报2026年上半年,越南18个省市报告有39人死亡,其中南部地区有20人死亡。动物暴发与2025年相比有所增加,影响24个省,主要起源于狗。河内6个公社记录了8只动物暴发,而胡志明市报告了5人死亡。卫生当局强调了该疾病的严重性,并指出没有受害者接受过 暴露后预防 治疗。尽管河内管理超过 360,000 只狗和猫面临挑战,但官员强调 疫苗接种 和动物贸易监管对于消除 狂犬病 至关重要。这种疾病仍然可以通过及时的伤口护理和 疫苗接种 来预防,但一旦出现症状,几乎总是致命的。
查看新闻五要素
时间
发表日期:2026-07-24。事件期间:2026年上半年(2026年1月-6月)。
地点与对象
受影响地区:18个省市(南部20人死亡,北部10人,中部和中部高原地区9人)。具体地点:河内(6个公社有8只动物暴发)、胡志明市(5人死亡)。受影响人群:人类(死亡39人)和动物(24个省/市暴发)。
事件
在人类和动物中确认了 狂犬病 暴发。2026 年上半年,越南在 24 个省/市记录了 39 例人类 狂犬病 死亡和动物 暴发。卫生当局报告,与 2025 年同期相比,狂犬病、受感染动物、动物死亡和扑杀有所增加。数十人在被动物咬伤后需要进行 疫苗接种 接触后治疗。
规模、影响与风险
人类病例:39 例死亡(南部 20 例、北部 10 例、中部/中央高地 9 例)。动物暴发:24个省/市。河内:8 只动物 暴发,每月约 5,000 次 狂犬病 治疗 咨询。胡志明市:5人死亡。风险:狂犬病 被描述为越南最危险的传染病之一,在动物之间持续传播。所有动物暴发都起源于狗。风险因素包括未接种疫苗的人群和接触狗/猫肉贸易。
应对、状态与不确定性
回应:卫生当局监测暴发;河内管理着约 360,000 只狗/猫。挑战包括监管动物运输、屠宰和贸易。预防措施:狗/猫的疫苗接种、伤口护理、暴露后疫苗接种。不确定性:39名受害者在被咬后均未接受疫苗接种或免疫球蛋白治疗;尽管有动物流通,河内自 2023 年以来没有记录到人类死亡。疾病状态:一旦出现症状,狂犬病 几乎总是致命的,但通过及时干预是完全可以预防的。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Vietnam records 39 rabies deaths in first half of 2026 - DTiNews

News Brief
Vietnam reported 39 rabies deaths across 18 provinces and cities in the first half of 2026, with southern regions accounting for 20 fatalities. Animal outbreaks increased compared to 2025, affecting 24 provinces, primarily originating in dogs. Hanoi recorded 8 animal outbreaks in 6 communes, while Ho Chi Minh City reported 5 human deaths. Health authorities highlighted the disease's severity, noting none of the victims received post-exposure prophylaxis. Despite challenges in managing over 360,000 dogs and cats in Hanoi, officials emphasized vaccination and regulation of animal trade as critical to eliminating rabies. The disease remains preventable through prompt wound care and vaccination but is almost invariably fatal once symptoms appear.
Original source text (English)
Quảng cáo của DTadsSunday, 26 July 2026Tiếng ViệtRSSVIETNAM TODAYBusinessSocietyEducationEnvironmentLIFESTYLETravelEntertainmentFood & DrinkCULTURE AND SPORTCultureSportCOMMUNITYMULTI MEDIACopiedVIETNAM TODAYVietnam records 39 rabies deaths in first half of 2026July 24, 2026 - 08:21 PMVietnam recorded 39 rabies deaths across 18 provinces and cities in the first six months of 2026, while outbreaks among animals continued to rise, health authorities said.said.Vaccinating dogs and cats against rabies helps reduce the risk of transmission to humans (Illustrative photo: Shutterstock).Vietnam recorded 39 deaths from rabies across 18 provinces and cities in the first half of 2026, according to the Ministry of Health.The ministry said southern Vietnam accounted for 20 deaths, while 10 were recorded in the north and nine in the central and Central Highlands regions.During the same period, rabies outbreaks in animals were reported in 24 provinces and cities. Compared with the first half of 2025, the number of outbreaks, infected animals, animal deaths and culls all increased.Nguyen Dinh Dang, head of the Hanoi Department of Livestock Production, Fisheries and Animal Health, said the capital had recorded eight animal rabies outbreaks in six communes since the beginning of the year. Dozens of people required post-exposure vaccination after being bitten by animals suspected of carrying the virus.Dang said rabies remained one of the country's most dangerous infectious diseases, posing risks throughout the breeding, transport, slaughter and trade of dogs and cats."Controlling vaccination, regulating the transport, slaughter and trade of dogs and cats, while avoiding the slaughter and consumption of dog and cat meat, may seem like small measures, but they are essential to eliminating rabies and preventing further deaths," Dang said.Hanoi is home to nearly 360,000 dogs and cats, he said, adding that managing pets, animal transport and slaughter remained a major challenge. According to official data, all recorded rabies outbreaks in animals have originated in dogs.The capital has not recorded a human rabies death since 2023, although the disease continues to circulate among animals.According to the Hanoi Centre for Disease Control, around 5,000 people seek medical consultation and preventive rabies treatment each month.Meanwhile, Ho Chi Minh City recorded five rabies deaths in the first six months of the year. Health authorities said none of the victims had received rabies vaccination or rabies immunoglobulin after being bitten by dogs or cats.Health experts stressed that rabies is almost invariably fatal once symptoms develop. Time
Publication date: 2026-07-24. Event period: First half of 2026 (January-June 2026).
Location and population
Affected regions: 18 provinces and cities (20 deaths in the south, 10 in the north, 9 in central and Central Highlands regions). Specific locations: Hanoi (8 animal outbreaks in 6 communes), Ho Chi Minh City (5 human deaths). Affected population: Humans (39 deaths) and animals (outbreaks in 24 provinces/cities).
Event
Confirmed rabies outbreak in humans and animals. Vietnam recorded 39 human rabies deaths and animal outbreaks in 24 provinces/cities in the first half of 2026. Health authorities reported increased outbreaks, infected animals, animal deaths, and culls compared to the same period in 2025. Dozens of people required post-exposure vaccination after animal bites.
Scale, impact and risk
Human cases: 39 deaths (20 south, 10 north, 9 central/Central Highlands). Animal outbreaks: 24 provinces/cities. Hanoi: 8 animal outbreaks, ~5,000 monthly consultations for rabies treatment. Ho Chi Minh City: 5 human deaths. Risk: Rabies described as one of Vietnam's most dangerous infectious diseases, with ongoing circulation among animals. All animal outbreaks originated in dogs. Risk factors include unvaccinated populations and exposure to dog/cat meat trade.
Response, status and uncertainty
Response: Health authorities monitoring outbreaks; Hanoi managing ~360,000 dogs/cats. Challenges include regulating animal transport, slaughter, and trade. Prevention measures: Vaccination of dogs/cats, wound care, post-exposure vaccination. Uncertainties: None of the 39 victims received vaccination or immunoglobulin after bites; Hanoi has not recorded a human death since 2023 despite animal circulation. Disease status: Rabies is almost invariably fatal once symptoms develop but entirely preventable with timely intervention.
